237	IMPORT_STRINGS = [
238	    'DEFAULT_GENERATOR_CLASS',
239	    'SERVE_AUTHENTICATION',
240	    'SERVE_PERMISSIONS',
241	    'POSTPROCESSING_HOOKS',
242	    'PREPROCESSING_HOOKS',
243	    'GET_LIB_DOC_EXCLUDES',
244	    'GET_MOCK_REQUEST',
245	    'SORT_OPERATIONS',
246	    'SORT_OPERATION_PARAMETERS',
247	    'AUTHENTICATION_WHITELIST',
248	    'RENDERER_WHITELIST',
249	    'PARSER_WHITELIST',
250	    'WEBHOOKS',
251	]
252	
253	
254	class SpectacularSettings(APISettings):
255	    _original_settings: Dict[str, Any] = {}
256	
257	    def apply_patches(self, patches):
258	        for attr, val in patches.items():
259	            if attr.startswith('SERVE_') or attr == 'DEFAULT_GENERATOR_CLASS':
260	                raise AttributeError(
261	                    f'{attr} not allowed in custom_settings. use dedicated parameter instead.'
262	                )
263	            if attr in self.import_strings:
264	                val = perform_import(val, attr)
265	            # load and store original value, then override __dict__ entry
266	            self._original_settings[attr] = getattr(self, attr)
267	            setattr(self, attr, val)
268	
269	    def clear_patches(self):
270	        for attr, orig_val in self._original_settings.items():
271	            setattr(self, attr, orig_val)
272	        self._original_settings = {}
273	
274	
275	spectacular_settings = SpectacularSettings(
276	    user_settings=getattr(settings, 'SPECTACULAR_SETTINGS', {}),  # type: ignore
277	    defaults=SPECTACULAR_DEFAULTS,  # type: ignore
278	    import_strings=IMPORT_STRINGS,
279	)
280	
281	
282	@contextmanager
283	def patched_settings(patches):
284	    """ temporarily patch the global spectacular settings (or do nothing) """
285	    if not patches:
286	        yield
287	    else:
288	        try:
289	            spectacular_settings.apply_patches(patches)
290	            yield
291	        finally:
292	            spectacular_settings.clear_patches()

163	
164	def perform_import(val, setting_name):
165	    """
166	    If the given setting is a string import notation,
167	    then perform the necessary import or imports.
168	    """
169	    if val is None:
170	        return None
171	    elif isinstance(val, str):
172	        return import_from_string(val, setting_name)
173	    elif isinstance(val, (list, tuple)):
174	        return [import_from_string(item, setting_name) for item in val]
175	    return val
176	
177	
178	def import_from_string(val, setting_name):
179	    """
180	    Attempt to import a class from a string representation.
181	    """
182	    try:
183	        return import_string(val)
184	    except ImportError as e:
185	        msg = "Could not import '%s' for API setting '%s'. %s: %s." % (val, setting_name, e.__class__.__name__, e)
